Record lookup: base + index × size (`FUN_80082070`, `FUN_800820b8`; ≥ 10000 → table 2). **NAME:** u32 count, count × u16 offsets relative to the string block that follows. **WWAZ:** 128 × u16 per character, low 12 bits = table-1 move, 0xFFFF = empty (DMX: slot 84 / 85 intro move 627 → clip 1801, slot 95 / 96 win move 734 → clip 1908). WPAR unpack = `FUN_8005cab8`.
- **Grapple victims:** victim clip = attacker clip + 1 when that clip's FZUI 7-bit field (+0x5C, same role as FFNY FZUI `b`) is ≥ 51 and no move uses it as its own clip: 451 of the 999 moves (324 used by a fighter). All grapples start from the same hold pose; victim = mirrored (x → −x, z → D − z). D taken from the FFNY viewer's partner distance of the matching FFNY animation (187 moves), else 7.
- **Same-animation test against FFNY:** 22 joint positions (FFNY viewer layout; Vendetta: hip, waist, chest, neck, head, L/R arm1 / arm2 / hand / arm_eff, leg1 / leg2 / foot / foot_eff) at 32 evenly spaced moments; signature = pelvis, chest, head, hands, ankles relative to the pelvis + the pelvis path; distance = RMS. Same animation ≈ 3.3-5 (the floor comes from the different joint definitions), similar 5-7, unrelated ≥ 7. Check: of 399 moves with the same name in both games, the nearest FFNY animation is the same-named one for 344. Used moves (755): 337 in FFNY, 62 similar, 27 same name but another animation, 329 new.
- **`Vendetta_Moves.html`** (artifact "Vendetta Moves"; since 11 October also marks the 39 moves that are FFNY Blazin's): every Vendetta move on DMX with the victim (blue) beside him, filters by fighter / FFNY status / kind, move card (clips, fighters and slots, damage, nearest FFNY animations with distances), all 2,824 animations as a second list, stars + notes in the browser and "Copy list for Claude". Built from the DMX clip viewer's data + `mv/moves.json` (`scripts/vwaza.py`, `vjoints.py`, `match.py`, `mkmoves.py`).

## DMX's Vendetta moves: Robert's notes (12 October 2026)

Robert's descriptions of DMX's (and others') Vendetta moves, for making DMX's FFNY moveset closer to Vendetta:
- **MADD CASH** = pin (Vendetta pins work like submissions: start pair, hold loop, kick-out pair, successful pin pair; a bar filled by button presses while the crowd counts to 3; at 3 the round ends with the pin). FFNY has no pins. **BLOOD N SWEAT** = pin too.
- **FOR MY DOGS** = arm submission. **PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR** = arm submission (FFNY equivalent?). **HARLEM HEIST** = body submission. **NINJA CHOKEOUT** = choke from behind on a victim in Vendetta's SITTING stunned state (after certain moves the victim sits on the floor stunned instead of lying; not in FFNY); **TWO FISTIN** = a strike on a sitting fighter.
- **RUFF RYDERS ANTHEM** ≈ (mirror of?) Santos' FFNY Blazin'. **DISHRAG** = DMX's rear Blazin'.
- **GET AT ME** = rear (back) wrestling grapple. **UNLEASHED** = running dropkick (at least one FFNY fighter has one). **CLEANIN' THA STREETS** = leg sweep (Rome's hard kick in FFNY; similar kicks as wake-up kicks).
- Ring / rope moves: **LIVIN' LARGE** turnbuckle dive; **STEALIN' HOME** run at the ropes + attack while the opponent is outside the ring = slide kick under the ropes; **REIGN OF PAIN**, **DOPE DROP**, **FACEPLANT** = leaps from the ring apron (outside the ropes, holding them) into the ring; **DEVILS ELBOW** = from the apron down onto an opponent on the floor outside; **MICROPHONE CHECK** = reversal of a grapple by an attacker holding the ropes; **ROUGH HOUSIN'** = hard grapple on a fighter standing on the apron; **BRINGIN THE NOISE** / **NEW JACK GRIND** (hard) = grapple by a fighter on the apron on a fighter in front of him; **GETTING PAID** = whip grapple catch.
- Taunts: **N62E2_AP** = DMX's Blazin' activation taunt (dog growl); **N6501_AP** = turnbuckle taunt; **N6602_AP** = rope-hold taunt.
- Requested ports (12 October): FOR MY DOGS, DISHRAG, GET AT ME, UNLEASHED (if no FFNY equivalent), PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR (if no close FFNY equivalent), HARLEM HEIST.

## Vendetta moves as normal FFNY moves: DMX's ports (12 October 2026)

**Built (emulated / checked with the game's decoder and loader, not yet tested in-game):** FOR MY DOGS, PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR, HARLEM HEIST (submissions, 10 animations each), GET AT ME (rear hard grapple pair), UNLEASHED (running kick, 1 animation), DISHRAG (rear Blazin'). None of them is in FFNY (moves viewer distances ≥ 10; FFNY's running dropkick TWO FOOTED FURY (move 595, slot 95 of 6 fighters, N1218AA) = Vendetta's TWO FOOTED FURY, not UNLEASHED). Library animations (in `ffny_allmoves`, 35 new): `V<move:04><pair><A|B>` for submissions (V01711A .. V01715B = FOR MY DOGS: opening pair, then the 8 hold animations), `V<move>__A/B` for pairs, `V0445__A` (UNLEASHED); all ≤ 8 characters (animtags keys).
- **Vendetta → FFNY data:** Vendetta move ids: FOR MY DOGS 171 (clips 995 / 996, tick record 825 → hold clips 200-207), PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR 497 (1585 / 1586, 857 → 456-463), HARLEM HEIST 528 (1641 / 1642, 879 → 632-639), GET AT ME 307 (1263 / 1264), UNLEASHED 445 (1496), DISHRAG 290 (1229 / 1230, rear: WAZA flags without 0x1000). DMX's Vendetta WWAZ (character 42): slot 16 FOR MY DOGS, 17 RUFF RYDERS ANTHEM, 24 GET AT ME, 25 DISHRAG, 58 UNLEASHED, 66 PERPETRATIN', 72 HARLEM HEIST (full list in the build log). **Vendetta's WAZA = FFNY's minus some fields:** dmg ↔ 00, 6-bit meter ↔ 02, 6-bit ↔ 03, 7-bit tank ↔ 04, 06 / 07 limbs the same, attack parts a0-a3 ↔ 08-0b (same bone numbers), Vendetta limb damage l0-l4 (3 bits) ↔ FFNY 10 / 14 / 18 / 1c / 20 (× ~13: 1 → 12, 3 → 40 in PAIN KILLER), points ↔ 36, 12-bit motion = skill clip, f1d = submission tick record (like FFNY 3c); tick records' motion = first of 8 hold clips (like FFNY 3a). **Submissions have the same 10-animation layout in both games** (PAIN KILLER: Vendetta 963 / 964 + 152-159 = FFNY N05691_A .. N05695_B; FFNY's FZUI table-1 numbering follows Vendetta's skill clip numbers in many places, e.g. FZUI 200-207 = FOR MY DOGS' hold frame data, used in FFNY by NICKLE N DIME's N06092A ..).
- **Ground submissions:** PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR and HARLEM HEIST start with the victim lying (root y 2.16), like FFNY's own GET BENT / SPOON FED / GHOST RIDAH / UNJUST CONVICTION / POWER OUTAGE (N3303x, N3314x, ...), which retail FFNY keeps in hard-grapple slots 11-20 (e.g. NORE, Masa, Solo); all four hard holds (D201 / D211 / D221) are standing, and the hold slot formula (0x80228C28: 1 / 11 + 5 for K + direction, rear 35-37, 0x80 → 52 / 53) has no ground case. How FFNY gets a lying victim into those moves was not traced. **(Traced 14 October: the lead-in is WAZA `29`, see the WAZA table and "Lead-ins and victim placement".)**
- **WAZA / FZUI of a port:** a copy of an FFNY template move's record (FOR MY DOGS ← PAIN KILLER 457 / tick 807; PERPETRATIN' ← SPOON FED 496 / 823; HARLEM HEIST ← GET BENT 495 / 822; GET AT ME ← UPTOWN SIDESLAM 509; UNLEASHED ← TWO FOOTED FURY 595), with 00 / 04 / 02 / 36 = Vendetta value × (FFNY template / Vendetta template) (e.g. FOR MY DOGS 20 → 40, GET AT ME 49 → 64, ground submissions 1 → 15), 06 / 07 and limb damage from Vendetta, attack parts from Vendetta (UNLEASHED 17, 18, 1, 2); tick record 00 likewise. FZUI = Vendetta FZUI mapped as for the Blazin's (a4c → none), speed 100, fields d / e / f17 / g / k from the template's record of the same role (strike: h / i / j too). UNLEASHED's attacker ends lying (b 51, like FFNY's FATAL FOOTWORK b 51 / f10 41): f10 set to 50 (first frame after the hit with root height < 3.0).
- **Animation writer (`ffnyanim.py`, rebuilt this session; the 11 October scripts were not in the hand-off):** body = 23 animated bones (FFNY bone ← Vendetta track from the moves viewer's VDATA `tr`) in one 0x0D node, 13 hair / face / pendant bones static (values of V0196__A) in a second; keys reduced greedily (nlerp error ≤ 0.25°), 16-bit, error-feedback quantisation. **0x0D node:** +0 type, +2 sid, +4 data ptr, +8 key-time ptr, **+0xC u16 C = number of listed times = number of rows**, +0xE u16 output word index per quaternion (N / 4 of them) (bone × 12 + 4); data = u16 N channels, u8 bits (4 / 8 / 16), u8 0, N × (min, scale, initial) floats, then C rows; value_0 = initial at frame 0, value_j = value_{j−1} + q × scale + min at time list[j−1]; 4-bit rows store two channels per byte, low nibble first. **The game never interpolates toward the LAST listed time** (retail ends the list with a 0 sentinel, the RJ port repeats the last key): written twice. Checked: game decoder (0x8010565C in the emulator) = the Python model exactly; RJ rebuilt this way ≤ 0.43° from Vendetta; face / hand tracks identical to the in-game-tested V0196__A. `_t` = 0x14 exactly like V0196 (every frame a key, shift 0, value = min + u16 / 65535 × range). `_d` = 0x0D Y rotation 0 → T (keys every 8 frames).
- **Facing (`_d` total turn T):** attackers and standing ends = root heading change (FFNY UPTOWN SIDESLAM −21 / 41 vs root −19.6 / 40); victims that end on the floor (b ≥ 51) = the Blazin' floor rule; **submissions: 0 for the opening and the hold loop** (FFNY's PAIN KILLER has 0 there although the root turns), **the hold-ending pairs = hip-line facing at the end + 15°** (fits 5 of PAIN KILLER's 6 values within 6°; FFNY's values are not the root heading). Values used: FOR MY DOGS 0, 0, 0, 0, −127, −180, −127, −180, −102, −180; GET AT ME 180 / −33; UNLEASHED 64; DISHRAG −6 / −15.
- **Camera / sounds:** submissions and GET AT ME get camera timetables for their attacker animations and animtags for all, copied from the template's entries (PAIN KILLER N05691_A .., SPOON FED N33141A .., GET BENT N33031A .., UPTOWN SIDESLAM N0728_A, TWO FOOTED FURY N1218AA) and retimed piecewise-linearly on the damage frame (f5) / landing (f14) and the end. DISHRAG (Blazin') uses RJ's rules (camera plan: slow 245 = shot change + 30, land 262, after 307, back 330, shakes 56 / 162; animtags pattern of RJ on its hit frames 54 / 160 and landing 261, type-6 window 299). camera.xml gets 13 new timetables (564 → 577 + DISHRAG): whether its list has a size limit is still not checked.
- **Tool: `ffny moves vport LIB "NAME" djv2.sod main_all.dat OUT.dat BANK BANK_out --fighter DMX --slots S --dol Start.dol OUT.dol [--camera ...] [--animtags ...] [--ko|--noko]`** (`vports LIB` lists them): first use appends the move to WAZA table 1 (+ its tick record for a submission; NAME gets the name and an empty name for the tick, like 799-825), one FZUI table-1 record per animation (2090 and up after RJ's 2088 / 2089), names them in Start.dol (grapplenames), copies the animations to the end of the bank and fills the slot(s); later uses find the move by name. `fighter_record` now also takes the start of an added character's internal name ("DMX" for "DMX alt fmzeyryx"). DISHRAG: `ffny moves blazinown LIB "DISHRAG" ...` (library "blazins" entry, rear).
- **Test run** (Robert's main_all.dat of 12 October, djv2.sod, retail Start.dol + cave, bigspace, motionlookup, grapplenames with RJ's names, Havoc's bank wres033 standing in for DMX's wres077): moves 827 (+ tick 828) FOR MY DOGS, 829 (+830) PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR, 831 (+832) HARLEM HEIST, 833 GET AT ME, 834 UNLEASHED, 835 DISHRAG; FZUI 2090-2124; bank 0.57 → 0.85 MB (retail maximum 0.94); all 552 embedded nodes with their header; **the game's main_all loader `FUN_801f9b50` (emulated) reads the result** (WAZA 836, FZUI 2125, DMX's slots byte-swapped correctly); motionlookup's rule gives every new animation its own FZUI record (submission hold animations through the tick record's 3a). Slots used in the test: 16 FOR MY DOGS (Vendetta's slot too), 17 PERPETRATIN', 18 HARLEM HEIST, 36 GET AT ME, 95 UNLEASHED, Blazin' all four directions (31-34 / 38-41).
- **In-game test 1 (13 October, Robert):** UNLEASHED (running K) works (hits, blockable, parryable, looks smooth) but after landing DMX gets up as from a floor state: long recovery, no real advantage on hit. FOR MY DOGS (hard K neutral) works: hold, ticks, submit with tap sounds, release aligned; damage goes to HEAD (Vendetta 07 = 0), Robert wants ARM. PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR (hard K right) works and looks close to Vendetta: FFNY plays its short takedown lead-in first (like NORE's ground submissions), snap into the hold not smooth, victim's hand outside DMX's hand = partner distance off; sounds, ticks, camera good. **HARLEM HEIST (hard K left): crash "Invalid read from 0x00000000, PC = 0x8003b308"** (ignore → no text over the limb bar; its 07 = 1 = body, which no retail submission uses); also misaligned at the start (DMX moving backward, victim forward). GET AT ME (rear hard P) aligned, ticks synced, but a kick hit sound and a whoosh heard in Vendetta are missing. DISHRAG works (rear Blazin', repeated use, KO, no crash; neck-snap impact sound could fit better); DMX (Havoc's grapple behaviour) cannot stay in a rear hard hold (a rear hard grab plays the rear move at once), so DISHRAG is reached through the front hold's turn-around.
- **Fixes after test 1 (13 October):** (1) **Crash 0x8003B308 = a null text pointer** in the UTF-16 → byte string copy (`lhz r9,0(r10)` with r10 = 0): HARLEM HEIST's victim limb 07 = 1 (body). **Retail submissions only target 0 head, 2 arm, 3 leg** (12 / 7 / 8 of them), so the submission meter has no body text. Library now: HARLEM HEIST 07 = 3 (leg, 1c), FOR MY DOGS 07 = 2 with its head damage moved to arm (opening 18 = 40, tick 18 = 12; Robert's request), and any port targeting body / fifth falls back to leg. (2) GET AT ME's sounds rebuilt on its own moments instead of UPTOWN SIDESLAM's: kick into the victim at 79 (its type-26 window start: impact 10 [1,2,...], voice 17, 58 / 62), whooshes 22 [195] at 76 / 82 (attacker) and 84 / 92 (victim spins fastest 83-100), landing 112 (big impact 10 [2,3,0,0,0,1000]), final hit 148. **(Corrected below: Vendetta does have a per-animation sound list, `Audio/defjam.aad`; this note was about the `Data` folder only)** section-4 events carry only face / hand / mouth (GET AT ME: 2 events each), `data/aud/` is the audience (crowd) models and animations; Vendetta's move sounds are presumably made by code from the frame data. (3) `vport --update` rewrites an installed move's WAZA / tick / FZUI records from the library (checked: only 827, 828, 831, 832 change; animtags rewritten with --animtags). (4) UNLEASHED's recovery = the floor state after f10 50 plus FFNY's shared get-up; tunables: FZUI 2122 `i` (recovery speed on hit, template 120) / `j` (on block, 100) and `f10` (when he is handed to the floor state).
- **Vendetta Moves viewer update (13 October):** submission moves (64, with field `tb` = first hold clip from the tick record) show a "Submission part" row: 1 opening, 2 hold loop, 3-5 (clips tb + 2 (n − 2) / + 1), HUD shows the part's clip numbers, "Copy settings for Claude" copies move, part, distance, sideways, faces-away. **Alignment method for PERPETRATIN' / HARLEM HEIST:** Robert finds the distance where SPOON FED (same animation in both games, aligned in FFNY) looks right (D_ref) and where PERPETRATIN' looks right (D_move); the victim's root z in all 10 animations then moves by −(D_move − D_ref) (viewer victim z = D − z_v).
- Scripts: `vendetta_moveport_scripts.zip` (vmain.py, vclip.py, vevents.py, ffnyanim.py, build_ports.py, emu.py / emu0d.py, ps2clip.py; README with the input paths). **PS2 track 0 = root rotation** (N0728_A: (0, −19.6, 0) = the root turn; submissions: the hold's root offset), not a facing track.

- **Vendetta's animtags.dat = `Audio/defjam.aad` (GC Vendetta root `Audio` folder, 13 October).** Format (big-endian): "AASD", a 0x108-byte header (version text), u32 entry offsets (2607 entries, sorted by name like FFNY's), entry = 8-byte name, u32 event count, events: **u16 frame, u16 n, u32 command, n × s32 parameters** (FFNY's layout with 32-bit fields). Frames are the clip / FZUI frames (A301: 17 / 45 vs FFNY 16 / 48; the last event of each long Blazin' clip is at its length − 1). The other Audio files: MAIN.VIV (sound banks + `audio/eventsys.aem`), flex.viv (speech + Events1-3.evt), speech.viv / .Nz, music.viv, volumes.txt (AEMS volumes).
- *(Superseded by "Sound events decoded" below; kept for the method.)* **Commands (from the 1406 animations both files have; FFNY re-authored them: 16,304 FFNY events vs 9,729 Vendetta, and FFNY gives sound IDs where Vendetta gives a loudness):** 9 [loudness 200-670] impact (→ FFNY 22 [402 / 98 / 207, 3, 0, 0, 0] one frame earlier), 31 [loudness] strike whoosh (→ 23 [195, ...]), 11 [n] effort voice (→ 25 [n]), 4 [n] grab / struggle voice (→ 17 [n]), 5 pain voice (→ 18 [1]), 56 [n] grunt (→ 37 [n]), 0 [n] hit on the victim (→ 10 [n, 3, ...] + 27 [1, 2, ...] + 62 [4, 0]), 13 [n] second impact layer (→ 10 / 27, 62 [2, 0]), 3 (→ 16), 6 [n] body hits the floor (→ 22 [402] + 37 [2] + 19), 0x10017 [n] face (→ 58 [n]), 29 (→ 33), 55 (→ 28), 10 (→ 70 [20, 0, 0, 0, 0, 17, ...]), 0x10002 / 05 / 09 / 0B Blazin' start cues and 0x10006 / 08 / 0C end cues (→ 1 [0] at frame 0). With these rules about 40 % of the converted events land within 3 frames of an FFNY event of the same command (FFNY moved / added many), so the result is Vendetta's sound timing in FFNY commands, not FFNY's own authoring.
- **Vendetta clip number → animation name, EXACT (13 October, from `Start-Vendetta.dol`, which was in Robert's hand-off zip all along; the earlier "not in this session" was wrong):** pointer table **0x8013CE14** (DOL file offset 0x139D94), 2824 entries = **821 base clips (index 0-820, A101 .. FF501) followed by 2003 skill clips (table 0x8013DAE8 = base + 821 × 4; skill clip i = entry 821 + i, N04272_A .. N5401_B)**. Read in the animation-sound driver at 0x80019150-0x800191A4 (per fighter: `FUN_800720e4` gives clip type / index / frame; type 0 base with index < 0x335 → name = table[index], anim id = index + 10000; type 1 skill with index < 0x7D3 → table 2 [index]). All 2824 names are unique. Written as **`ffny_tools/ffny_vnames.json` (2003 skill names, replaces the rebuilt one below; same format: JSON list indexed by clip number) and new `ffny_tools/ffny_vnames_base.json` (821 base names)**. Coverage: 1782 skill and 579 base names have a defjam.aad entry. The six ports' clips all have exactly the names the rebuild had given them (checked: FOR MY DOGS 995 / 996 / 200-207, PERPETRATIN' 1585 / 1586 / 456-463, HARLEM HEIST 1641 / 1642 / 632-639, GET AT ME 1263 / 1264, UNLEASHED 1496, DISHRAG 1229 / 1230), so their sounds stay as built. Script: `findtab.py` / `vdol.py` / `ports_names.py`.
- **Vendetta's defjam.aad lookup (`FUN_800e7d68` key, `FUN_800e7e5c` binary search over the offsets at aad + 0x108):** key = the first 8 bytes of the name as they are, **no upper-casing, no underscore handling**. So an aad entry whose spelling differs from the DOL name never plays in Vendetta. 246 aad entries match no clip name: 237 are cut animations (N11xxAA / N12xxAA runners, `_AC` / `_BC` / `_AP` variants, N06Exx, N33331A-N33335B, ...), plus a "ZZZZ" end marker; **9 are spelling variants of real clips:** N06223_A .. N06225_B (DOL: N06223A .. N06225B = FOR MY DOGS holds 3-5; **all six entries are empty**, so those holds have no Vendetta sounds either way and keep the template sounds), N1127_AA (DOL: N1127AA, 4 footsteps); N1003A / N1003B exist next to the correctly spelled N1003__A / N1003__B, so they are spare copies. `vshow` / `vcopy` find a variant spelling automatically.
- **How Vendetta plays the events (`FUN_800e5dc0` per fighter per frame, read from code):** events whose frame equals the current frame are copied into a 16-slot per-fighter queue (fighter audio block 0x801EEB38 + n × 0x168, queue at +0x40; fighter −1 = the one with the shortest queue); `FUN_800e5b4c` drains it. **Commands ≥ 0x10000 are game-side** (`FUN_800e530c`): 0x10001 .. 0x10017 (music / camera / crowd cues; 0x10017 [n] stores n at fighter +0x38 = the face state), and **0x10018 [c] as the FIRST event of an entry = character override**: if c equals the fighter's value, the driver switches to the entry named `XEAC%04d` (unused in the retail aad). Bit 0x10000000 on a command = play only while the fighter's counter at 0x801EC4B8 + n × 4 is < 1 (cmd 0x10000004, 9 uses). **Commands 0-73 are not interpreted by code at all: they are sent (`FUN_800e63bc`) as event numbers to EA's audio event system (`audio/eventsys.aem` in MAIN.VIV), which decides the sample.** The game adds a per-fighter value to some: **9 (impact), 31 (whoosh), 6 (body hits the floor) and 5 (pain) get the venue's floor type** (fighter +0x18 = 1 when +0x30 is set, else field +0x28 of the stage table 0x801A322C (stride 0x2C, entries name the venue sound banks WHSFX, SANCTSFX, DRGNSFX, LEFRKSFX, FACE1SFX, BRDGESFX, LUDASFX, JUNKSFX, DJAM1SFX, CLUBSFX, FACE2SFX, DJAM2SFX; value 2 or 3)), 13 and 73 get fighter +0x2C; 9 and 31 are skipped once the counter at 0x80440580 reaches 3; only 9, 11 and 31 play while the flag byte 0x8044052D is clear. This fits the conversion above (9's parameter = loudness, the sample chosen by floor type). The exact sample per event number is in eventsys.aem (not decoded yet).
- *(Superseded by the exact table above; kept for the method.)* **Rebuilt clip names (1657 of the 2003 skill clips)**, made before the DOL table was used: FFNY's record-name table (0x80301E30) is sorted by name in blocks and FFNY's FZUI table 1 keeps Vendetta's order with insertions, so Vendetta and FFNY FZUI records were aligned on identical frame data (unique matches, longest increasing run, equal-length runs between anchors, gaps filled with the defjam.aad names that fall between the anchor names, and a global alignment for the rest), every name checked against the clip length (last event ≤ length), submission holds named from their opening (`Nxxxx1A` → holds `Nxxxx2A` .. `Nxxxx5B`) when the hold block belongs to one submission only. **Our ports:** FOR MY DOGS N06221A/B + holds N06222A .. N06225B (N06223 .. N06225 have no events in Vendetta), PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR N27641A/B + N27642A .. N27645B, HARLEM HEIST N33701A/B + N33702A .. N33705B, GET AT ME **N07J0_A / N07J0_B** (kick hits the victim at 91, he lands at 116), UNLEASHED N1257AA, DISHRAG N07H3_A / N07H3_B. Not checked in-game yet.
- **Tools:** `animtags vlist defjam.aad [PREFIX]`, `animtags vshow defjam.aad NAME|CLIP` (raw events with meanings + the converted events), `animtags vcopy defjam.aad NAME|CLIP animtags.dat OUT.dat DST [--keep] [--shift N]` (--keep keeps DST's cue commands 1 / 65 / 70), **`animtags libports ffny_allmoves.json animtags.dat OUT.dat`** (writes every ported move's entries from the library; nothing else changes). `build_ports.py` now takes each animation's sounds from defjam.aad (`vsounds`) and keeps the template's retimed events only where Vendetta has none (FOR MY DOGS holds 3-5, PERPETRATIN' V04973A); it keeps FFNY cues 1 / 65 / 70 and, for GET AT ME, the 195 whooshes (Vendetta has no whoosh event there). Library JSON entries have `sounds_from`. Analysis scripts: `vaud/work/` (aad.py, cmp.py, stat*.py, align.py, vnames.py, dp.py, combine.py, final.py, vconv.py) in `vendetta_moveport_scripts.zip`.

## Sound events decoded: Vendetta's and FFNY's sound systems (13 October 2026)

- **Both games send animtags / defjam.aad commands to EA's audio event system; the command number is the event.** Vendetta: `Audio/MAIN.VIV` holds `audio/eventsys.aem` (+ 33 `.abk` banks, `.ast` streams). FFNY: `audio/sfx0-3.viv` hold `sfx/DefJam2_eventsys.csi` / `.a1c` (named events) and newer "ABKC" banks; FFNY's sample data was re-encoded (22050 Hz where Vendetta often used 32000), so samples cannot be matched byte for byte.
- **eventsys.aem (Vendetta, big-endian):** "AE" v0x15, project hash 0x00B5EC04 (same in every .abk), counts 935 / 314 / 74 / 41, section offsets 0x20 / 0x57C8 / 0x6B68 / 0x80A8. Section 3 = **74 event records of 32 bytes** (event = command 0-73): u16 parameter count, pointer to the parameter list (8 bytes each: pointer to that parameter's routing records, count), pointer + count of rows (section-2 records, 16 bytes: u16 bank id, u16 module id = the module the event starts in that bank), two more lists. Section 1 = 935 routing records of 24 bytes (u32 kind, u32 value (1000 / 3), u16 bank, u16 module, u32 module parameter id, −1, −1): event parameter → a parameter of the target module (impact: loudness → id 0x89; floor type → the bank's floor input 0xCA54A / 0xCA55A / 0xCA56A). Section 4 = 41 global parameters (the volume groups), each a list of section-1 routings.
- **Banks (.abk "AB" v9):** header u32 size, hash, module count (at 0x0C), 0x38 = first module record; **bank id u16 at 0x14** (WCommon1 0x61E7, WCommon2 0x0003, venue banks WHSfx 0x49A1, LudaSfx 0x0309, LefrkSfx 0x5315, ClubSfx 0x5372, DrgnSfx 0x587E, JunkSfx 0x587F, BrdgeSfx 0x5880, Djam1/2 0x5881/2, Face1/2 0x5883/4, SanctSfx 0x5885, grunt banks Gr* 0x68xx-0x6Axx, specials Sp* 0x1244 Cork, 0x1245 Punch, 0x2F9A Kick, 0x301B Boob, 0x4279 Ass, 0x42A3 Chest). Module record 28 bytes: u16 id, u16 kind, u16 node count, 0, u32 0, u32 template, **u32 file offset of the module's node graph**, … Module = node graph: node = u8 type, u8 inputs, u8 outputs, 0, u32 offset of its output connection list ((u16 node, u16 port) entries), parameter values; node handlers / sizes = table **0x801A98E8** (entry = handler, byte0 = node size in the file). **Type 4 = sample player** (node +0x2C = sample reference: 0x4000 | index into the bank's own BNKb, 0x8000 / 0xC000 = other sources; it calls the player through 0x80209F78 + 0x18 / 0x20 / 0x24), type 0x14 / 1 = inputs by id, 5 = envelope, 8 = random (RNG 0x80105BB8, state 0x8020AA0C), 0x1F = ramp. Samples: "BNKb" v5 (u16 count at +6, header size, data size, table of u32 offsets each relative to its own slot) → EA "PT" headers (tags: 0x85 samples, 0x84 rate (default 22050), 0xA0 codec 0x12 = GameCube DSP ADPCM, 0x88 data offset from the BNKb start, 0x8F = the 16 DSP coefficients).
- **Engine calls (Vendetta Start.dol):** init `0x800F9ED8(aem)`, bank register `0x800FA1A4(bank, 0, 0)` then slot table `[0x80209F78 + 0x0C][slot]` = bank and bank+0x30 → two handles, event `0x800FA8A8(&{cmd, p1, p2, …})`, tick `0x800FB2BC(rate)` (runs every module whose rate matches); the game's own queue `0x800E63BC` → `0x800E8390` → function pointer r13−0x7C3C. Volume groups: records of 0x48 bytes at 0x801A57FC (name, u32 index, u32 default): MASTER 0, PUNCH 1, FACEHIT 2, CRWD* 8-11, FE_SFX 14, BONECRUNCH 15, BODYCONTACTS 16, HANDSLAP 17, MATFALL 18, MATFALLSUPER 19, CONCRETEFALLS 20, FEETMAT 21, SFX1 22, FEETCONCRETE 23, GRUNTS 24, FOLEYFX 25, BLAZINSFX 26, WHOOSH 27, KICK 28, GRAPPLE 29, MOMENTSFX 30, SPECIAL 31, LOCATIONSFX 32, POINTSMOVE 33, SFX2 34, AMBIENCE 35, DIRTFALLS 36, SFX3 37, SFX4 38, SFX5 39, FEMALE_GRUNTS 40.
- **Emulated result (unicorn, Vendetta DOL + eventsys.aem + WCommon1/2, a venue bank, GrWhite, Sp*; scripts `vaud/emu/` in `vendetta_sound_engine_scripts.zip`), what each Vendetta command plays:** 0 [1 / 2-3] = **punch / kick hit** (WCommon2 module 0x006D, KICK / PUNCH, samples 23-25 / 26-28); 3 [1-3] = **bone crunch** (0xD260, samples 35-37); 4 [1-4] = **grapple body contact** (0xD2DF, BODYCONTACTS / GRAPPLE, two samples per value); 5 = **hand slaps the floor** (venue module 0xD3A0, HANDSLAP; the game adds the floor type); 6 [size 1-4, floor] = **body fall** (venue 0xD3E1 MATFALL / CONCRETEFALLS, outdoor venues 0x59ED DIRTFALLS); 7 = venue SFX1 (0xD581); 8 = WCommon2 SFX1 (sample 41); **9 [weight, floor] = footstep** (floor 1 → WCommon1 0xD603 FEETMAT, indoor venue floor 2 → venue 0xD63B FEETCONCRETE, outdoor floor 3 → 0x7901; weight changes only pitch / volume); 10 = WCommon2 SFX4 (sample 38); **11 [1-4] = whoosh** (WCommon1 0xD70E WHOOSH, four samples per size); **13 [1-6] = grunt** (the fighter's voice bank, module 0x6A60 in GrWhite; the per-fighter value picks the bank; 1-4 several samples, 5 and 6 one each); 17 = nothing; 28 = **face hit** (0xD8ED FACEHIT); 29 [1-2] = **foley** (0xD8DB); 31 [weight, floor] = **light footstep** (0x3A95 / 0x3AB9, plays in about half the trials); 55 [1-2] = WCommon2 SFX5 (samples 29 / 30); 56 [1-2] = WCommon2 SFX2 (44 / 45, a landing sound); 57 / 58 / 68 / 69 / 70 / 71 = specials Cork / Punch / Kick / Boob / Ass / Chest; 73 [1-3] = second grunt set (0x69E6). The earlier labels "9 = impact", "11 = effort voice", "13 = impact layer", "4 = grab voice", "5 = pain voice", "56 = grunt" were wrong.
- **FFNY's commands = its named events (read from code):** FFNY registers event names into handles at 0x8036259C (`0x801F2A58(handle, name)`, list at 0x802A9FB4: Audio_engine_start, Global_Ducker_Event, …, Punch_Kick, Groin_Punch, Chops, Face_Hits, Bone_Crunch, Body_Contacts, Hand_Slap, Falls, Rail_Hit, Turnbuckle_Hit, Feet, Feet_cycle, Rope, Whooshes, Transition_sfx, Grunts, Grunts_cycle, …). The animtags queue (0x80437558, filled by `FUN_80034900`) is drained into the dispatcher **`FUN_80035394`**, a compare tree on the command: **10 Punch_Kick, 12 Groin_Punch, 14 Chops, 15 Face_Hits, 16 Bone_Crunch, 17 Body_Contacts, 18 Hand_Slap, 19 Falls, 20 Rail_Hit, 21 Turnbuckle_Hit, 22 Feet, 23 Feet_cycle, 24 Rope, 25 Whooshes, 27 Grunts, 28 Grunts_cycle, 33 / 42 Foley_Fx, 34 / 43 Blazin_sfx, 36 Muscle, 37 Land, 46-49 Weapon_Grab / Strike / Break / Drop, 50 Special_Kick, 51 Special_Punch, 52 Special_Cork, 53 Special_Boob, 54 Special_Ass, 55 Special_Chest, 64 Environmental_Smash, 67 Surface_Hit, 68 / 69 Blood_Gush, 70 Omni_Event, 71 Burn_Yell, 72 Foot_Pillar, 73 Falling_Wood**; each case clamps its values (e.g. Feet: p0 0-1000, p1 1-30, p2 0-65535, p3 0-32767, p4 0-1000) and triggers the event with `0x801F327C(handle, values)`. Handled before the queue (`FUN_80034900`): 58 (0x3A) = face, 59 / 60 = intro / win speech, **62 (0x3E) = `FUN_800BCEE0` on the fighter (not a sound; probably a hit effect / rumble)**. So **FFNY's "22 [402, 3, …]" is Feet with foot sound 402**, not a generic "sound by ID" (earlier notes, e.g. the Blazin' section, call it that).
- **New Vendetta → FFNY conversion (`ffny_animtags.vconv`, 13 October; old rules kept as `vconv_old` / `--old`):** each Vendetta sound becomes FFNY's event of the same kind, with the values and frame offset FFNY itself uses next to that Vendetta event in the 1406 shared animations (they now co-occur strongly: footstep ↔ Feet, whoosh ↔ Whooshes 1-4, hand slap ↔ Hand_Slap [1] 53 %, fall ↔ Falls + Land, hit ↔ Punch_Kick): 9 [w] → 22 Feet [402 if w ≥ 335, 98 if ≥ 200, else 207, 3] at f − 1; 31 → 23 Feet_cycle [195, 2] at f − 2; 11 [n] → 25 Whooshes [n] at f − 1; 0 [n] → 10 Punch_Kick [1 / 2, 3] at f − 1 + 62 [4, 0]; 3 → 16 Bone_Crunch [1 / 3]; 4 [n] → 17 Body_Contacts [n]; 5 → 18 Hand_Slap [1]; 6 [n] → 19 Falls ([1,0] / [2,0] / [3,2]) + 37 Land [2]; 13 / 73 [n] → 27 Grunts [n ≤ 4, 2 / 8] at f − 2; 28 → 15 Face_Hits [1]; 29 → 33 Foley_Fx; 56 → 37 Land; 55 → 28 Grunts_cycle (SFX5, unsure); 10 → 70 Omni_Event (SFX4, unsure); specials 57 → 52, 58 → 51, 68 → 50, 69 → 53 [1], 70 → 54, 71 → 55. Compared with the old rules: Vendetta's grunt (13) no longer becomes a punch sound + grunt, hits (0) no longer get an extra grunt, falls (6) no longer get an extra footstep, specials are converted. **`animtags vrebuild LIB defjam.aad NEWLIB`** re-converts the ported moves' stored sounds (28 animations of the six ports + DISHRAG; template parts and cues 1 / 65 / 70 kept); not tested in-game yet.
- **Automatic Vendetta sounds for ported moves (13 October, evening):** `ffny_tools/defjam.aad` is now shipped with the tools (the path can be left out of vlist / vshow / vcopy / vrebuild). `ffny_animtags.apply_vendetta(entry)` takes each animation's Vendetta clip from the library entry (`vendetta.clips`, in `anims` order; Blazin's A / B = clips[0] / clips[1]) → name (ffny_vnames.json) → defjam.aad → vconv; animations without Vendetta events keep the template sounds, cues 1 / 65 / 70 are kept, `sounds_from` is filled. It runs in `animtags libports` (unless `--stored`) and in `moves vport` / `blazinown` / `blazin --animtags`, so future ports need no hand-made sound list. **REPRESENTIN' JERSEY** (clips 1045 / 1046 = N06E8_A / N06E8_B) had been built from FFNY template sounds before defjam.aad was found (empty `sounds_from`); it now gets Vendetta's own sounds too. Its earlier, in-game-tested sounds are kept in the entry as `animtags_before_vendetta`. Watch in the test: Budden's voice line after the last strike may have been the template's attacker Grunts (27 [2, 2] at 190); Vendetta's attacker animation has no grunt there (the grunts are on the victim).
- **In-game (13 October, Robert's animtags-newest.dat):** REPRESENTIN' JERSEY's V0196__A / B are exactly the Vendetta conversion (+ the kept FFNY events 1 at 0 / 213 and 65 [3] at 240 on A); Robert: sounds very similar to Vendetta, but Budden says an after-Blazin' taunt line that Vendetta does not have. **FFNY command 65 is not a camera / shot cue: the animtags dispatcher (case 0x41 at 0x80035C2C) runs the silence check `FUN_80033520(-1)` and then the speech / announcer request `FUN_801D36B8`**, so 65 [3] at 240 (kept from the FFNY template pattern) is almost certainly that line. Command 1 is queued but has no case in the sound dispatcher. FFNY's "Taunt" / "Char_Speech" event handles are only registered / released (0x800271xx / 0x8007A2xx), never triggered from code. Not yet tested: removing 65 from V0196__A. **Confirmed from FFNY's own data (Robert's idea):** retail animtags.dat has `65 [3]` on the attacker animation of essentially every Blazin' (N06xx_A / N07xx_A, one per move, near the end; 117 animations have command 65, the BT2_01-04 ones with `[4]`), and on PUMMEL HORSE's N07J8_A at 214 (a Vendetta Blazin' animation that FFNY gives Crow and others as back hard grapple, slots 36 / 37; Robert hears the after-Blazin' taunt even outside Blazin'). Vendetta's N07J8_A / N06E8_A have nothing like it. So **65 [3] = the after-Blazin' taunt speech request**, an FFNY addition.
- **Submission crunch (13 October, Robert: FOR MY DOGS / PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR holds crunch in Vendetta, not in the port):** Vendetta's bone crunch in a submission is in the animations: command 3 usually on the victim's 4th hold part (HARLEM HEIST N33704B, SPOON FED N33144B, PAIN KILLER N05694_B; FOR MY DOGS' N06224B entry is one of the empty misspellings, PERPETRATIN' has no command 3), and **command 55 (WCommon2 SFX5 samples 29 / 30: 0.9-1.4 s, bright, sharp attack, crackle-like; bone crunch samples 35-37 are similar, grunts darker) at the start of the hold animations** (N06222B 2, N27641B 60, N27642A 26, N27644A 2, N33702A 12). The fight code sends no crunch itself (its direct audio calls are events 27 / 59 / 61 = front-end and points sounds). FFNY puts Grunts_cycle / Muscle there, which is why FFNY's holds do not crunch. **Rule changed: 55 → 16 Bone_Crunch [1 / 3] at f − 1** (was 28 Grunts_cycle). Not yet tested in-game. Vendetta Moves viewer: partner distance slider now −20 .. 20 (was 0 .. 20).
- **Sound timeline (13 October, Robert's idea): `Vendetta_Move_Sounds.html` (Vendetta Moves viewer + timeline) and `ffny_anim_sounds.html` (FFNY animation viewer + timeline)**, built by `ed/build_v.py` / `ed/build_f.py` from `ed/timeline.js` + `ed/tl.css` + `ed/data.json` (`ed/prep.py`: defjam.aad events, clip names, port names from the library, Vendetta sample groups from the emulator sweeps, 107 DSP-ADPCM samples, 1.3 MB). Lanes: attacker / victim FFNY events (from a loaded animtags.dat, kept in the browser's IndexedDB; FFNY name defaults to the port name or the Vendetta name and can be typed), and the Vendetta attacker / victim events (read-only reference). Click = select + hear, drag = move, double-click = add, Del / arrows / Ctrl+Z on the focused timeline, inspector (frame, type, values, hear, duplicate, remove), "Fill from Vendetta" (the vconv rules in JS), sounds play while the animation plays (FFNY events preview with Vendetta's sample of the same kind and the chosen floor; FFNY's own samples are not decoded), edits kept in localStorage, "Download edits" → `animtags_edits.json` (`{"format":"ffny-animtags-edits","entries":{NAME:[[frame,cmd,[values]],...]}}`). **`animtags apply animtags_edits.json animtags.dat out.dat [--lib LIB]`** writes them; `--lib` stores them as `sounds_custom` in the ported moves' library entries, which `apply_vendetta` (libports / vport) uses instead of converting again. Seen in the FFNY viewer: in PAIN KILLER's hold (N05692) FFNY puts Muscle where Vendetta has the crackle (55).
- **Robert's in-game check of the crunch rule (13 October):** FOR MY DOGS: the crunch (Vendetta 55 at frame 2 of the hold loop N06222B) plays where DMX puts the arm back, i.e. at the loop restart, not in sync; PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR / HARLEM HEIST: crunch plays as DMX starts to stretch (in sync), but not the crunch sound he wants. To be tuned by Robert in the timeline.
- **Alignment values from Robert (Vendetta Moves viewer, 13 October):** HARLEM HEIST part 1: victim faces away, distance 16.8, sideways −0.5; parts 2-5: faces away, −2.0, 1.8. PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR part 1: faces away, 15.5, −0.3; parts 2-5: faces away, 6.0, 2.3. SPOON FED's stored default is distance 18.5 facing the attacker, so the correction is a 180° turn plus a shift, not a shift only; waiting for SPOON FED's own values per part (the FFNY-aligned reference) before writing new victim animations. Robert also notes PERPETRATIN's FFNY takedown lead-in leaves the victim on his back with the head away from DMX, while the armbar starts with the victim on his stomach, head toward DMX (one cause of the jump); where FFNY picks that lead-in is not traced (possible fix: a template whose lead-in leaves the victim on the stomach).
- **Resolved 14 October:** see "Lead-ins and victim placement" below.

## Lead-ins and victim placement for ground submissions (14 October 2026)

- **Robert's SPOON FED lineup** (Vendetta Moves viewer; same animations as FFNY's N3314x, which look right in FFNY): part 1 distance 17.5, sideways 0.0, victim toward DMX; parts 2-5 distance 1.5, sideways 1.0, toward.
- **Lead-in = WAZA `29`** (see the WAZA table): the template's value was copied into the ports, so both PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR and HARLEM HEIST played SPOON FED's / GET BENT's D255 (victim on his back, head away).
- **How the game places the parts (checked on retail data):** every animation is played from the fighter's current place; at its end the place moves by the root's end − start (SPOON FED opening → hold: fit of N33142A frame 0 onto N33141A's last frame = pure shift (−9.0, 12.5), victim (7.7, 3.0), no turn). Retail lead-ins join their opening without a jump: fitting the opening's first frame onto the lead-in's last frame leaves 0.2-0.4 units (SPOON FED, GHOST RIDAH), 1.1 (CUBAN NECKTIE's attacker). With Robert's SPOON FED values this gives the **hard hold distance H = victim toward the attacker at 7.84** (yaw −177.5) during the lead-in, and the victim's place relative to the attacker when the opening starts, per lead-in (viewer terms): D253 yaw −8, (−2.5, 15.0); **D255 toward, (0, 17.5)**; **D257 toward (176.8°), (−0.51, 14.90)**; **D259 away (−1.3°), (−1.47, 17.70)**. Robert's predicted part-2 values from his part-1 values and the root shifts (SPOON FED (1.4, 1.9) vs his (1.0, 1.5)) agree.
- **Hand contact check** (Vendetta joints, FFNY layout; attacker hands to any victim joint): PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR only works with the victim **toward** DMX (part 1 from frame 24 and the whole hold loop: DMX's hands on the victim's left hand, 0.2-1.5 units; with "faces away" ≥ 4 units, the hands touch nothing). So Robert's "faces away" for PERPETRATIN' was taken as toward (his distances 15.5 / −0.3 match where D257 leaves the victim within 0.6, and his words "on the stomach, head toward DMX" fit D257). HARLEM HEIST works with "faces away" (contact 1.0-1.4). The part-2 placement predicted from part 1 fits even better than the typed part-2 values (PERPETRATIN' mean 0.36 vs 0.48, HARLEM HEIST 0.74 vs 1.19), so **one correction per move is used for all five victim animations**.
- **Changes:** PERPETRATIN' ARMBAR `29` = 3 (**D257**, Robert's choice), victim animations V04971B-V04975B turned +3.2° and shifted (−0.24, −0.59); HARLEM HEIST `29` = 4 (**D259**, the only lead-in leaving the victim on the stomach with the head away), V05281B-V05285B turned +1.31° and shifted (0.95, −0.93) (correction = (opening placement of the lead-in)⁻¹ · Robert's part-1 lineup). Expected joins (mean joint distance between the lead-in's last frame and the opening's first): PERPETRATIN' victim 7.2 → 0.7 (attacker 0.4 → 1.2, same as CUBAN NECKTIE's own), HARLEM HEIST victim 7.2 → 1.4 (attacker 0.4). Both earlier in-game reports fit the old state: PERPETRATIN' with D255 is placed 2 units too far (Robert: "victim's hand outside DMX's hand"), HARLEM HEIST with D255 is turned 180° (Robert: DMX moving backward, victim forward).
- **Checked:** the changed banks decode (Python model and the game's 0x0D decoder 0x8010565C in the emulator, max difference 3e-6) to Vendetta's joints with the correction applied (≤ 0.03 units); only the ten victim animations' bytes changed; 552 embedded nodes with their header. Library (`ffny_allmoves`) and its JSON updated (`waza` `29`, `placement` note), so a fresh `vport` / `vport --update` gives the same. Files for Robert: `main_all_leadins.dat` (= his modded_root main_all.dat of 13 October with moves 829 / 831 `29` changed, 2 bytes) and `wres077_leadins.ord/.orl/.mnl` (his wres077 from modded_root wres_all.viv with the ten victim animations changed). **Not yet tested in-game.**
- Scripts: `leadin_scripts.zip` (`fv.py` reads the FFNY viewer's joint data, `vj.py` Vendetta joints in the FFNY layout, `rig.py` yaw + shift fits, `align.py` the lead-in placements, `vplace.py` the first version of the transform with a forward-kinematics check; paths as in the Vendetta port scripts).
- **Assumption to watch in the test:** a constant shift inside an animation is honoured by the game (retail animations do start away from the origin, e.g. G701 / G702 / N4112AA), and the place moves by end − start. If instead the victim drifts by about 1 unit each time the hold loop repeats, the game moves by the end position only, and the shifts must be redone.

- **The exact sample per FFNY value (e.g. what Feet 402 vs 98 sounds like) is not known yet:** that needs FFNY's own engine run the same way (see "Open questions" 0). Vendetta's samples per command are exported as WAV (`vendetta_sounds/`, index.tsv) for listening.
- **Name table check:** the rebuilt `ffny_vnames.json` (before the DOL table) had 1488 of its 1657 names right and 169 wrong; none of the wrong ones were in the six ports.

## Blazin's and porting Vendetta Blazin's (11 October 2026)

- **How a Blazin' is found:** WPAR bytes 56-59 (right, left, up, down stick) = Blazin' value v (0-81), copied to fighter +0xBB8. Move = WAZA table-2 record **142 + v**; animations = blazin.viv entries **2v** (attacker) and **2v + 1** (victim) = global motion 10709 + 2v / + 1, frame data FZUI table-2 records **709 + 2v / 710 + 2v** (`FUN_80203100`: base 0x2C5, count 0xA4 = 164, flag table 0x803F6B50; `FUN_80203018` flags 2v and 2v + 1 for both players' values, so only those Blazin's are loaded). WAZA table 2 has 256 records (224-255 empty, names only to 223); FZUI table 2 has 873.
- **Front / rear (see the correction in the own-bank section):** values **0-56 = front Blazin's (no turn), 57-81 = rear Blazin's (from a front hold the attacker first turns the victim around)**; the start check below in detail (`FUN_80228cc0`: front (+0x91A & 0x200) uses WWAZ slot 38 + dir and needs v + 0x279E ≤ 0x27D6; otherwise slot 31 + dir and needs v > 56; the same split in the preload `FUN_802026f0`). djv2.sod names agree: 52-55 "CP W/WBK/WBS/WBM FRONT", 78-81 "... REAR"; 47-51 "Hero Street Fighting / Martial Arts / Submissions / Wrestling / Kickboxer". WAZA `2c` = 805326848 for 0-56, 805322752 for 57-81. A fighter's four directions can mix front and rear values; a direction whose value does not fit the grapple position does nothing.
- **Owners (retail main_all):** each named fighter has one value in all four directions (Ludacris 1, Doc 4 ... Santos 46, Bonecrusher 57 ... Jervis 77; Joe Budden 59 = rear). 0 (Crow) is in 63 placeholder records; 47-50 only record #76, 51-54 #77, 55-56 #78 (+ Shadowy Figure 56), 78-81 #79.
- **blazin.viv** (`FUN_800b00dc` with "blazin", CBlazinAnimationData; retail GC file 2,882,646 bytes, 329 entries, **all RefPack**): `blazin.mnl` (u32 164, u32 0, '.'-separated names, e.g. N06L4_A, N06L4_B, ...; value v = names 2v / 2v + 1: 38 = N06J8_A/B, 52 = N0683_A/B, 59 = N07J7_A/B), then `NAME.ord` + `NAME.orl` per animation, each **its own bank** (symbol `__AnimationBank:::a` in every entry) holding `NAME_d`, `_f`, `_h`, `_s`, `_t` (0x13 + 0x12 body, 0x14 root, one 0x0D `_d`, two 0x19 nodes in `_f` and in `_h`; info 60 fps). Archive file names are matched case-insensitively (N06I0 is stored as `N06i0_A.ord`; its objects use the mnl spelling). **Confirmed with the game's decoder:** entries 2v / 2v + 1 are the Vendetta clips of the matching Vendetta Blazin' (SKULL FRACTURE 1111 / 1112, HELL RIDIN' 1277 / 1278, COVER CHARGE 1039 / 1040: root position within 0.02, body within 1-6.5°), length = Vendetta length + 1.
- **`_d` in retail Blazin's:** linear from 0 to a total turn T (Y rotation). For 58 of 78 ported animations T equals the root's heading change (local x axis projected on the floor, unwrapped) modulo 360, often with whole turns dropped (COVER CHARGE attacker: root 400°, T 520°; HELL RIDIN' attacker: T −360°); the rest are hand-picked values ±180° or a few degrees off. The port uses the unwrapped root heading change.
- **Face / hand tracks of FFNY's Vendetta ports (`_f`, `_h` 0x19 nodes):** node = u16 0x19, u16 sid (0xC241 face / 0xC51C hand), u16 key count, u16 0x00EA (first node) / 0x01EA (second), ptr pose numbers (bytes), ptr key frames (u16, 60 fps); 16 bytes. Keys = the clip's section-4 events of that channel, same frames. **Face:** Vendetta face value n → (first node, second node) = 1 (4, 0), 2 (4, 0), 3 (5, 6), 4 (6, 14), 5 (8, 5), 6 (5, 7), 7 (5, 6), 8 (4, 8), 9 (11, 14), 10 (9, 5). **Hands:** first node = left hand (event bits 15-19), second = right hand (bits 10-14), pose number = value − 1. No mouth track. Rebuilding the 78 retail Blazin' animations this way gives identical face / hand tracks for 146 of 156 (the rest were retimed by hand in FFNY).
- **Vendetta → FFNY Blazin' timing is copied 1:1** (checked on SKULL FRACTURE = FFNY value 38, Manny): FFNY FZUI fields = Vendetta FZUI a30 → hit_start, a34, a38, a3c, a40 → f5 (damage frame), a44-a54 → f6-f10 **except a4c, which FFNY leaves at 2047**, a58 → a, a5c → b (victim landing posture), a60 → f14, a64 → c, w0-w3 → w1-w4, speed → h / i / j; FFNY adds d = 3, g = 1. Window types: 25 on the attacker, **26 on the victim = the damage ticks**, 6 = the short window around the landing.
- **39 of Vendetta's 91 Blazin's are FFNY Blazin's** (attacker + victim FZUI equal or 1 field off), e.g. SKULL FRACTURE = 38 Manny, VERTABUSTER = 30 Warren G, HELL RIDIN' (Vendetta rear) = **59 Joe Budden**, LAP DANCE = 36 Shaniqua, STEEL GRIP = 31 Erick Sermon; the 5 Hero / CP front values and 3 of the 4 CP rear values are Vendetta Blazin's too. List: `bz/blz_matches.json`; the moves viewer now marks them "FFNY Blazin'". REPRESENTIN' JERSEY (Vendetta front Blazin', Budden's) is not among them.
- **FFNY move animation details used for the port** (checked by decoding SWINGSET SLAM N0418__A/B, = Vendetta skill 827/828, with the game's decoder): FFNY frame f = Vendetta frame f (both 60 fps), frames = Vendetta length + 1; `_t` = bone 0 translation (word 8) exactly Vendetta's root position (victim starts at (0, 8.64, 0) like Vendetta's); `_s` = local rotations of 36 bones (`build_rj.BONES`; retail 0x13 + 0x12 nodes, 0x0D works); `_d` (sid 0x1400) = one 0x0D group at word 4, a pure Y rotation rising linearly from 0 to the move's total turn (N0418__A 300°, __B 70°; derived from the root heading, which gives 307° / 70°); `_f` (sid 0xC241) / `_h` (0xC51C) = two 0x19 pose-track nodes each (the emulated decoder cannot run 0x19 outside the game). Info block `00000001 00010001 3c303030` (60 fps).
- **REPRESENTIN' JERSEY port (installed into Robert's retail blazin.viv, not yet tested in-game):** Vendetta move 196 (skill 1045 / 1046, 319 / 348 frames, damage 80, tank 24, points 500, front) → library animations **V0196__A / V0196__B** (320 / 349 frames, body error ≤ 0.3° against Vendetta, root exact, `_d` turn 100° for both, face / hand tracks from its events with the tables above; hair, pendant and face bones at rest). FFNY data: WAZA damage 80, health tank 45 (FFNY's usual partner of 80; Vendetta had 24), points 500, the rest kept from the replaced value; FZUI from Vendetta as above (attacker f5 211, windows 10-126 / 237-284 type 25; victim f5 211, f9 195, b 69, f14 199, windows 241-243 type 6, 14-36 / 50-69 / 130-154 type 26). Test build: value **52** (CP W FRONT = Vendetta COVER CHARGE; in retail only placeholder record #77 has it) → blazin.viv entries N0683_A / N0683_B replaced (only those 4 entries changed; archive check clean), Joe Budden's right-stick Blazin' = 52 (his other three stay 59 = HELL RIDIN' from behind).
- **In-game test 1 (11 October, REPRESENTIN' JERSEY on Budden vs Blaze, value 52):** animation aligned, damage ticks at the right time, camera freeze right before the stunner lands (Cover Charge's camera, whose slow-motion frame 180 happens to equal Representin' Jersey's a4c). Wrong: (1) after the move the victim flips 180° when the floor animation takes over; (2) no hit sounds on the ticks (the slot still has Cover Charge's sound events).
- **Victim facing after a Blazin' (fix for 1):** the floor animation that follows is placed with the victim's final `_d` facing. In FFNY's 39 Vendetta-port victims that facing is the body axis hip → head on the floor **+ 180° for odd landing postures `b` (61, 67, 69, 71) and + 0° for even ones (52, 60, 70)** (38 of 39 within ±5°), not the root turn. Representin' Jersey's victim (b 69): root turn 100°, body axis 100° → facing −80° (was 100°, which caused the flip). `build_rj.floor_facing`; attackers keep the root turn.
- **Blazin' cameras are in `motion/camera.xml`**, one timetable per attacker animation name (the victim has none), rows `frame, shot, b, kind, c, d`: kind 0 = new shot, `shake` (size S / M / L), `slow` (freeze; params like B1 / M1), `finish` (the next frame: finishing shot), `after`, `back`. FFNY's Vendetta-port Blazin's follow Vendetta's data: **slow ≈ Vendetta FZUI a4c** (SKULL FRACTURE 186 / slow 185, STEEL GRIP 210 / 208; the field FFNY drops from FZUI), a shot change 30 frames before it, **big shake at the victim's f14 + 1** (landing), `after` 15-50 frames later, `back` ≈ 20 frames before the end, and shakes 2 frames after the start and the end of the victim's damage-tick windows (SKULL FRACTURE: windows 26-80, 101-125, 150 → shakes 28, 82, 103, 127, 152 exactly). `ffny moves blazin ... --camera camera.xml OUT.xml` retimes the replaced Blazin's timetable this way (keeps its shots): Representin' Jersey on N0683_A = 1, shakes 16 / 38 / 52 / 71 / 132 / 156, shot 150, slow 180, finish 181, landing shake 200, after 245, back 300.
- **Blazin' sounds = `audio/animtags.dat`** (entries by animation name, attacker and victim; frames at 60 fps for moves). In FFNY's Vendetta-port Blazin's the victim gets **command 10 = impact (hit sound), params [1, 2, 0, 0, 0, 0] / [1, 3, ...] / big [2, 3, 0, 0, 0, 1000]** at the start and the end of its damage-tick windows and at the landing (SKULL FRACTURE victim: 26, 81, 100, 125, 148, 195 for windows 26-80, 101-125, 150 and f14 196), with **62 [4, 0]** one or two frames later (62 [2, 0] twice after the landing) and **58 [3] at each hit, 58 [0] about 9 frames later** (on both fighters); attacker: **22 [id, 3, 0, 0, 0] = sound effect by id** (195 swing, 402 / 98 / 207 / 598 / 415 impacts and slams), **25 [n] effort voice** shortly before hits, 27 [2, 2, ...] just before the freeze, **1 [0] at frame 0 and again ~15 frames after the landing** (probably the Blazin' music cues), 65 [3] around the 'after' shot; victim voices 17 / 18 / 19, 37, 50 [0, 0, 0] at the landing. Representin' Jersey on N0683_A/B used COVER CHARGE's events, so only its big impact (10 [2, 3, ...] at 210) sounded. `port_blazin.tag_plan` now writes both entries: a hit per tick-window start / end (14, 36, 50, 69, 130, 154) with the victim and attacker events above, and SKULL FRACTURE's finish events moved to Representin' Jersey's landing (f14 199) and type-6 window (241); installer option `--animtags animtags.dat OUT.dat`. Not yet tested in-game.
- **New Blazin' values without replacing one (82+), what it needs:** the loaded-flag table 0x803F6B50 is exactly 164 bytes (next variable at 0x803F6BF4), so it must move (3 references: 0x80203030, 0x802030C4, 0x80203118, plus the address returned by `FUN_80203100`); the count 0xA4 in `FUN_80203100` and in the memset before it (it also sizes CBlazinAnimationData's arrays, `mulli 0x18`); blazin.mnl count + new entries (the loader takes NUM from the mnl); FZUI table 2 grows past 873 records (709 + 2v); WAZA table 2 has room up to v = 113 (records 224-255 empty); the front / rear rule (v ≤ 56 front) in `FUN_80228cc0` (0x80228E40-0x80228E60) and `FUN_802026f0` (0x80202770-0x80202798) would make every new value a rear one unless patched (e.g. a per-value front / rear byte table in a cave). Not yet checked: whether the FZUI loader / global-motion lookup bound-checks table 2 against a constant, and the djv2.sod Blazin' category (names / shop / created fighters).
- **Tools:** `ffny moves blazins ffny_allmoves [djv2.sod]` lists ported Blazin's and the 82 values (front / rear, owner); `ffny moves blazin ffny_allmoves "REPRESENTIN' JERSEY" djv2.sod main_all.dat OUT.dat blazin.viv OUT.viv --value 52 --give "Joe Budden" [--dirs right]` writes the two animations under the names blazin.mnl gives value v (RefPack if the originals were), WAZA 142 + v, FZUI 709 + 2v / 710 + 2v, and the --give fighters' Blazin' bytes; refuses a front Blazin' on a rear value (or the reverse) unless `--force`. `ffny moves extract LIB ANIM OUT [NEW_NAME]` writes one animation as its own bank. Porting scripts (Vendetta side): `bz/s/port_blazin.py LIBIN LIBOUT <Vendetta move ids>` (uses `build_rj.py`, `mbank.py`); `ffny moves build` would rebuild the library without the ported animations. Run on the retail blazin.viv (11 October): replaced entries decode correctly with the game's decoder, other entries byte-identical, main_all changes only those records. The installer writes the bank symbol `a` like retail.

## Expansion study: more animations, moves, Blazin's and fighters (11 October 2026)

**In-game test 2 (REPRESENTIN' JERSEY):** every damage tick now has a hit sound, camera angles and shakes good, the victim no longer flips after the move (facing rule confirmed). Port considered working.

Limits found in Start.dol (addresses checked with capstone on Start-FFNY.dol; helper scripts `bz/d.py` (disassembly with resolved lis/addi), `bz/scan.py` (all code references into an address range), `bz/cmp2.py` (compare-immediate search)):

- **main_all.dat tables are file-driven**: `FUN_801f9b50` reads every WAZA / FZUI / NAME count from the file; the file itself is loaded at its own size (`FUN_80245c10`, path at 0x80096014). So **new moves (WAZA table 1, move id 12 bits ≤ 4095), names and new global motions (FZUI table 1, ids < 10000: `FUN_802021c0` splits at 0x270F)** need no code change; a fighter bank can take any number of added animations (`moves add` + `motionlookup`). Only memory per bank matters (largest retail bank 0.94 MB; 4 banks loaded).
- **Fighter records (WPAR + WWAZ, = movesets): hard limit 141** — the loop `cmpwi r28, 0x8C` at **0x801F9DA0** and the 16-byte per-record pointer table at 0x803F601C-0x803F68EC (inside the struct at **0x803F5FAC**: +0x00-0x6C table pointers / counts, +0x70 + 16 × record: WPAR, +0x74, +0x78 WWAZ size, +0x7C WWAZ), immediately followed by other variables (0x803F68EC, 0x803F6B48 ...). Code references to the struct base 0x803F5FAC: 0x801F9B6C, 0x801F9B80, 0x80202280, 0x802022A8, 0x8020CAA0, 0x8021D53C, 0x8021D584, 0x8021D614, 0x8021E278, 0x8021E2F8 (+ 0x80202400 direct 0x803F6028). Records 0-85 roster, 86-140 created-fighter style templates; only one other place treats records > 85 specially (0x8020309C, Blazin' values copied from the created fighter's choices). Banks are `wres%03d` = record + 1 (format strings 0x802B7C74 ..., loader `FUN_800af048` from `FUN_80097008`, 4 players) → fine up to 999.
- **Free memory for moved tables:** OSInit sets the heap start (ArenaLo) to the constant **0x804624C0 / 0x804624E0** (0x80271C18, 0x80271C50, 0x8027677C; stack 0x804424C0-0x804624C0). Raising it (e.g. + 0x10000) gives a static block at 0x804624C0 for new tables, filled by a new DOL data section (DOL sections 2-6 and 12-17 are unused) — the usual GameCube way to add memory; costs that much heap.
- **Blazin's:** see the Blazin' section (164 animations, flag table 0x803F6B50 full, count 0xA4 at `FUN_80203100`, front ≤ 56 rule). Alternative that removes the limit: **per-fighter Blazin's from the fighter's own bank** — `FUN_8021d4a4` (0x8021D4F0-0x8021D578) takes the Blazin' move from WAZA table 2 record 0x8E + value and ignores the WWAZ slot except its KO bit; the motion is 10709 + 2 × value at 0x802027F8 (preload) and in the Blazin' grapple code. A patch "value ≥ some marker → use WWAZ slot 31-34 / 38-41 like a normal grapple (table-1 move, own animation, own global FZUI)" would let every fighter carry his own Blazin' in his bank with no blazin.viv change (front / rear then from the slot used).
- **Select screen / front end:** the shared list at **0x80439DCC holds 128 entries** (filled without a bound at 0x8003E628 and in `FUN_8003ea78` at 0x8003F028; ~40 readers check `cmplwi idx, 0x7F` between 0x8004BA4C and 0x8004F694; 70 code references; next variable 0x80439FCC) → about 128 selectable entries (characters + outfits). Retail djv2.sod has 75 Character records (FE_Id up to 98, 70 distinct movesets). Select-grid squares use frame FE_Id + 1 / FE_Id + 150 → FE_Ids ≥ 149 collide unless the apt's 150 is raised and frames appended. Not yet checked: profile / save storage of per-character unlocks, other FE_Id-indexed tables.
- **Plan for ≥ 2× roster (≈ 150 characters / 140 movesets):** (1) ArenaLo + new data section; (2) move the 0x803F5FAC struct there, loop count 0x8C → N (records 141+ = new movesets, wres142+); (3) move the 128-entry list and raise the 0x7F bounds; (4) thumbAll offset; (5) Blazin' per-fighter patch or the 82+ value patch; (6) tools: `fighterslot own` for records ≥ 141, `addchar`, `fepics`. Each step testable on its own.

## Own-bank Blazin's and per-move unblockable / unparriable (11 October 2026)

- **Loading note:** blazin.viv is not loaded whole: `FUN_80203018` flags only the animations 2v / 2v + 1 of the Blazin' values of the fighters in the match, and `FUN_800b00dc` loads just those.
- **Patch `ownblazin` (built, emulator-tested, not yet tested in-game):** a fighter whose WWAZ Blazin' slot is filled does that move from his own bank; all retail Blazin' slots are empty (0xFFFF), so retail behaviour is unchanged. Slots (corrected below): **31-34 = used from a front hold, 38-41 = from a rear hold**, + direction (0 right, 1 left, 2 up, 3 down, from +0x434 in `FUN_80228cc0`). Changes (no cave space):
  - `FUN_8021d4a4` 0x8021D50C-0x8021D578 rewritten: reads the slot's WWAZ entry first; filled → the normal-move path (table-1 WAZA move, `0xFBC` = player × 1000 + bank index, KO bit from the entry; the tail still sets +0x926 bit 0 = Blazin'); empty → the shared path as before, which now also stores the shared motion 10709 + 2v in `0xFBC` (last 3 instructions in a helper at 0x80227B70).
  - Blazin' motion at 0x80227B6C and preload at 0x802027D4: always use `0xFBC` (victim = + 1).
  - Trigger check `FUN_80228cc0` 0x80228E34-0x80228E60: own-bank (`0xF54` ≠ −1) → allowed; else the old front / rear value rule (crxor of the 0x200 flag and v > 56).
  - Emulated with Robert's main_all: own slot → move 799, motion player × 1000 + bank index, damage 80; empty slots → value 59 (motion 10827, damage 100), identical to retail apart from `0xFBC`; with `motionlookup` bank index n / n + 1 map to the new frame data 2088 / 2089.
- **Correction after the in-game test (12 October): how a Blazin' really starts.** The hold flag +0x91A 0x200 = holding from BEHIND (set from the grab geometry in `FUN_8022705c` / `FUN_8021e16c`, and by grapple moves that end in a rear hold, `FUN_8021d1d4`; the hold turn at 0x80227D94 toggles it). `FUN_80228cc0` (Blazin' input) decodes the slot's move (front hold: WWAZ slot 31 + dir, rear hold: 38 + dir; shared: table-2 move 0x8E + value) and returns 3 only for front hold + value > 56 or rear hold + value ≤ 56; 3 → intro motion 10200 (front hold: the attacker turns the victim around) / 10202, then the Blazin'. A **front Blazin' (≤ 56) from a front hold returns 0 on purpose**: the hold handler (0x802279D4) then sees the decoded move's **WAZA `2c` bit 0x1000** (set on values 0-56: 2c = 0x30005000; 57-81: 0x30004000) and plays intro 10162 (0x27B2), then the Blazin' without turning. So 'front / rear Blazin'' = WAZA 2c bit 0x1000, not the slot.
- In-game results: first `ownblazin` (any own-bank Blazin' allowed from any hold) put Budden's own Blazin' through the turn-around path from a front hold (misaligned, no camera / sounds; one crash 'Invalid read from 0x98, PC = 0x800F6798' on the second try; cause found 12 October: missing node headers in the ported animations, see "Animation banks"). **`ownblazin` v2:** the start check compares the decoded move's 2c bit 0x1000 with the rear-hold flag (identical to retail for all 82 values × both holds, emulated); own-bank slots: **31-34 used from a front hold, 38-41 from a rear hold**; `blazinown` fills both and sets 2c bit 0x1000 for front Blazin's. Old version: `--revert oldownblazin`.
- **`ffny moves blazinown LIB "NAME" djv2.sod main_all.dat OUT.dat wresNNN wresNNN_out --fighter X --dol Start.dol Start_out.dol [--dirs right] [--camera camera.xml OUT.xml] [--animtags animtags.dat OUT.dat]`** (`--dol` added 12 October, see the next section): first use **appends a new WAZA table-1 record (826 for the first one; count + 1, + 1 on the last of the 49 header group counts, which nothing in Start.dol was found to read)**, its name in NAME table 1 (count + 1) and **two new FZUI table-1 records (2088 / 2089)** (chunks kept 4-byte aligned); later uses find it by name. **Records 799-825 look unused (no WWAZ uses them, unnamed) but are the submission tick records linked through WAZA `3c` — never reuse them.** Copies the two animations to the end of the fighter's bank (attacker, then victim) and fills his slot(s) (move | 0x8000, bank index, global). Camera timetable (copy of N0683_A's shots, retimed) and animtags entries under the library names (V0196__A / V0196__B). Emulator: `FUN_801f9b50` reads the longer tables fine. Not checked: whether camera.xml's list has a size limit (it counts its entries).
- **Unblockable in retail:** `FUN_80231128` (hit) gets an attack-kind flag from the caller: 1 light strike (`FUN_8022e414`), 2 hard strike / whip strike, 4 wake-up attack, 8 (0x8022F130), 0x10 ground strike, 0x200 / 0x10 (0x80232ACC), 0x400 (0x80209F30, skips reactions); **0x40 / 0x80 / 0x100 = weapon attacks (0x802323EC, 0x802326A8, 0x80232894)**. Guard (state 2, sub-state 2) and block stun (3) block unless flags & 0x1C0 (except when the victim's +0x8D4 bit 0 is set, state 2 only), or +0x918 bit 8.
- **Unparriable in retail:** `FUN_8021f144` allows a parry only against attacker **states 0x45 (light strike), 0x46 (hard strike), 0x48, 0x6C-0x6E**; wake-up attacks (0x47), whip strikes, weapons and everything else are not parryable by state. No WAZA field is involved.
- **WAZA field `3e`** (12 bits, decoded to fighter +0xFB2) is 0 in every retail move and read nowhere (checked with the decoder in the emulator and a code search; `38` is NOT free: `FUN_8021d1d4` reads it as the state after the move). **Patches `unblockflag` (FUN_80231128 0x8023139C-0x80231410, includes `blockstaggerflag`) and `unparryflag` (FUN_8021f144 0x8021F16C-0x8021F198):** 3e bit 1 = unblockable, bit 2 = unparriable. Emulated: 512 block cases identical to `blockstaggerflag` except unblockable moves; parry identical except flagged moves. Set with `charstats setwaza ... <move> 3e=1 / 2 / 3`.

## Own-bank Blazin': crash, camera / sounds / voice line (12 October 2026)

- **In-game report (ownblazin v2, Budden RJ on right):** lines up and no longer turns Blaze around, but no unique camera shots, no strike sounds, no Budden end-of-Blazin' voice line (heard when RJ replaced shared value 52), and doing it twice → crash "Invalid read from 0x00000098, PC = 0x800f6798". Crash = missing node headers, see "Animation banks" (fixed by `fixbank`, automatic now).
- **Camera, sounds and voice are looked up by NAME, from a static table in Start.dol, not from the bank.** `FUN_80202fa0(flag, idx)`: flag 1 → FZUI table-1 record idx, pointer table **0x80301E30 (2088 entries, idx ≤ 0x827)**; otherwise table 2 at 0x8030108C (873 entries, idx ≤ 0x368); out of range → **"(none)"** (0x80303EFC). Called from the per-player packet writer at 0x801fb408 (flag = fighter +0x84, idx = fighter +0x8C, the record `FUN_802021c0` / motionlookup stores; the name goes to packet + 0x74; packet array 0x8035EA28, stride 0xC8) and from two debug prints (0x8020bbd0, 0x8020c020). The table is referenced only there (one `addi` at 0x80202FC4). Consumers of the name: animtags player `FUN_80034054` (uppercased 8-character key, binary search `FUN_800339a0`) = strike sounds / voice events, and the grapple camera `FUN_800b9314` / `FUN_800b944c` (camera.xml timetable `<name>`). RJ's frame data are records **2088 / 2089 → "(none)"**, so neither its animtags entries (V0196__A / V0196__B) nor a camera timetable could ever be found. Emulated: flag 1 / idx 24 = N05012_A, 2087 = N5401_B, 2088 = (none). (Note: about 876 table-1 record pairs are used by no WWAZ slot and no WAZA record — cut content, e.g. 24 / 25 N05012_A/B; not needed now.)
- **In-game result (12 October, Robert's own DOL + grapplenames + repaired wres031): REPRESENTIN' JERSEY on Budden works from light and hard grapple — no turn-around, its camera shots, strike sounds, Budden's voice line after the last strike, and repeated use without a crash.** So the end voice line is an animtags / name-driven event too.
- **Patch `grapplenames` (cave; tested in-game):** the "(none)" exit of the flag-1 path (0x80202FB0, `lis r3,0x8030`) becomes `b 0x80001EB0`: idx − 0x828 < 20 → name slot at **0x80001F10 + 12 × (idx − 2088)** if its first byte is not 0, else "(none)" as before (13 instructions, 0x80001EB0-0x80001EE4). So **records 2088-2107 can have names** (20 slots, 11 characters max.). Set with `grapplenames=REC:NAME[,REC:NAME...]`; `--status` lists them; `--revert grapplenames` removes hook + table. Emulated with the patched DOL: 0 / 24 / 2087 unchanged, 2088 = V0196__A, 2089 = V0196__B, 2090-2108 and 0x7FFF = (none), table 2 unchanged.
- **`blazinown` now needs `--dol Start.dol Start_out.dol`** (with grapplenames applied): after the slots it writes the animation names of the two records (A for the attacker record, B for the victim's) into the slots, and writes camera.xml / animtags entries under the names the DOL gives the records (V0196__A / V0196__B). If the animations are already in the bank but without node headers, it repairs the bank (writes `wresNNN_out`). Tested on Robert's data: RJ stays move 826 / FZUI 2088-2089, main_all and animtags.dat unchanged (the V0196__A/B entries were already right), bank repaired, DOL names 2088 / 2089; second Blazin' test (fresh move 827, records 2090 / 2091 → slots 2 / 3) works; camera path checked on a test camera.xml (timetable V0196__A = N0683_A's shots, retimed: 1, shakes 16 / 38 / 52 / 71 / 132 / 156, shot 150, slow 180, finish 181, landing 200, after 245, back 300).
- **Capacity / growing it:** 20 slots = 20 records; a grapple or Blazin' uses 2 (attacker, victim), RJ uses 2088 / 2089 → 18 left = 9 more two-part moves. Growing: the slot area 0x80001F10-0x80002000 is bounded by gamecorex at 0x80002000 and the cave is nearly full (free: 0x80001AE4-0x80001B00, 0x800022C8-0x80002300, 0x80002700-0x80002740, 0x80002FC0-0x80002FE0, and 0x80001EE4-0x80001F00 small). Options: (a) 9-byte slots (8 characters, which is all the animtags key uses) → 26 records in the same area; (b) a pointer list instead of slots, with the strings in the other holes; (c) give new records the names of unused retail record pairs (about 876 pairs are used by no WWAZ slot or WAZA record, e.g. 24 / 25 = N05012_A/B; camera / animtags entries then under those names) — hundreds more without DOL space, but the names are not the animation's; (d) a larger new DOL section (ArenaLo study). Other limits: WAZA table-1 move IDs are 12 bits (≤ 4095; 827 now), WWAZ globals u16; camera.xml list size and animtags.dat size not checked.
- **Delivered 12 October:** `Start.dol` = retail + motionlookup, unblockflag, unparryflag, ownblazin (v2), cave, grapplenames with 2088 = V0196__A, 2089 = V0196__B (Robert's DOL had exactly the first four); repaired `wres031.ord/.orl/.mnl` (Budden with RJ, animations 143 / 144, 0.70 MB) to put into wres_all.viv. main_all.dat / animtags.dat unchanged. camera.xml: Robert runs blazinown with `--camera` on his own file (not in the hand-off files). To test: camera shots, strike sounds, the end voice line, and the Blazin' several times in a row. If the voice line is still missing with sounds working, it is not an animtags event (look at what the shared-value path does at the end of a Blazin', e.g. a value-based voice request).

## Start.dol patches (`python ffny.py dolpatch IN OUT names...`; `--status`; `--revert names`)

Value patches (`name=value`) are applied after the named ones. "cave" = needs the extra code area.

| Patch | Effect | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `motionlookup` | order-independent animation → motion lookup (needed after slot edits / `moves add`) | tested |
| `wallnodizzy` | full-stun fighter doing the standing wall get-off: flag cleared, stun 90 % (needs motionlookup) | tested |
| `wallchance=X` | wall fall-off chance per hit (retail 0.075) | tested |
| `nokd06/08/17/36` | remove one state from the forced-knockdown list | test patches |
| `runhit1/2/3` | a hit on a RUNNING fighter gives flinch / short / long stagger instead of knockdown (needs motionlookup) | tested |
| `runstrikenokd` | running strikes (and weapon running strikes) knock down by stun only (> 70 %), not by low health (table 0x802E4B0C rows 1-3 = row 0) | tested |
| `enderstagger` | combo enders no longer knock down when every chain hit connected | tested |
| `timewinhealth`, `crowdtime=M:SS` | time-over decided by health; Crowd Vote time limit (+ HUD) | tested |
| `noclinchshove` | no RUN / GRAPPLE shove in the clinch hold | tested |
| `blockwallstick` | block stagger 0x73 can wall-stick (needs the C301 data fix) | tested |
| `blockstaggerflag` | strikes with WAZA `25` ≠ 0 put a guarding blocker into 0x73 | tested |
| `ownblazin` | Blazin's from the fighter's own bank when his WWAZ Blazin' slot is filled (needs motionlookup) | v2 tested in-game (with grapplenames + repaired bank: fully working) |
